[0m$ [0msed -n '1,200p' .skilled/commands/assets/local-skilled-alignment/check-release-coverage.cjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODULE: Check Release Coverage
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
'use strict';

// Judges a scope against the two root documents an operator actually reads: the
// open root release notes and the root README. A skill the branch adds, or a
// version bump it ships, shows up in the diff, but a reader learns about it only
// when those documents name it. A check that reads the touched lines alone
// cannot see that gap, so this one reads the documents whole.
//
// The open release note is the highest version under the root changelog folder,
// compared as dotted numbers rather than as text. Four-part versions ship in
// order, so v4.0.0.10 is newer than v4.0.0.9 and a lexical sort inverts the two.
//
// An id is matched as a whole word: a hit inside a longer id or a path fragment
// does not announce it, and the match is case-insensitive because a document may
// lead a sentence with the name.
//
// Usage: check-release-coverage.cjs --scope FILE [--repo DIR]
// --repo defaults to the current directory.
// Exit 0 every required id is described, 1 a required id is missing, 2 usage or
// input error, including no root release notes to read.

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. IMPORTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const { fail, readJson } = require('./alignment-lib.cjs');

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const TAG = 'check-release-coverage';
const USAGE = 'Usage: check-release-coverage.cjs --scope FILE [--repo DIR]';
const NOTES_DIR = '.skilled/changelog/skilled';
const NOTES_NAME = /^v([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)*)\.md$/;
const SKILL_CHANGELOG = /^\.skilled\/skills\/(?:.+\/)?([^/]+)\/changelog\/v[0-9.]+\.md$/;
const SKILL_DOC = /^\.skilled\/skills\/(?:.+\/)?([^/]+)\/SKILL\.md$/;
const NAME_LINE = /^name:[ \t]*(.+?)[ \t]*$/m;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. ARGUMENTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const opts = { scope: null, repo: process.cwd() };
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i += 1) {
    const [flag, inline] = argv[i].split(/=(.*)/s, 2);
    const value = () => {
      const v = inline !== undefined ? inline : argv[++i];
      if (!v) fail(TAG, `${flag} needs a value\n${USAGE}`);
      return v;
    };
    if (flag === '--scope') opts.scope = value();
    else if (flag === '--repo') opts.repo = value();
    else fail(TAG, `unknown argument: ${argv[i]}\n${USAGE}`);
  }
  if (!opts.scope) fail(TAG, `--scope is required\n${USAGE}`);
  return opts;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 4. THE OPEN RELEASE NOTES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function versionParts(name) {
  return NOTES_NAME.exec(name)[1].split('.').map(Number);
}

// A shorter version pads with zeroes, so v4.0.0 and v4.0.0.0 rank equal.
function compareParts(left, right) {
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(left.length, right.length); i += 1) {
    const diff = (left[i] || 0) - (right[i] || 0);
    if (diff !== 0) return diff;
  }
  return 0;
}

function newestName(names) {
  let newest = null;
  for (const name of names) {
    if (newest === null || compareParts(versionParts(name), versionParts(newest)) > 0) newest = name;
  }
  return newest;
}

/**
 * Read the open root release note. A folder with no versioned note is a checker
 * error: reporting every id as missing would blame the change for a document
 * that is not there.
 *
 * @param {string} repo - Resolved repository root
 * @returns {{file: string, text: string}} Absolute path and contents
 */
function openNotes(repo) {
  const dir = path.join(repo, NOTES_DIR);
  const names = fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((name) => NOTES_NAME.test(name)) : [];
  if (names.length === 0) fail(TAG, `no root release notes under ${NOTES_DIR}`);
  const file = path.join(dir, newestName(names));
  return { file, text: fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8') };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 5. REQUIREMENTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function escapeRegExp(text) {
  return text.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
}

// A whole-id hit: neither neighbour may extend it into a longer id or a path
// fragment, and the start and end of the document bound it on their side.
function appearsIn(text, id) {
  return new RegExp(`(?<![A-Za-z0-9_-])${escapeRegExp(id)}(?![A-Za-z0-9_-])`, 'i').test(text);
}

// The frontmatter name of the added SKILL.md, or null when it names none or the
// file is not on disk. The folder name is the fallback, so a document that is
// not readable yet still has to be announced under its folder.
function frontmatterName(repo, rel) {
  let text;
  try {
    text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo, rel), 'utf8');
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  const block = text.match(/^---\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n---(?:\r?\n|$)/);
  if (!block) return null;
  const line = block[1].match(NAME_LINE);
  if (!line) return null;
  const name = line[1].replace(/^(['"])(.*)\1$/, '$2').trim();
  return name || null;
}

/**
 * Walk the scope's added paths and record which ids each root document must
 * name. An id is kept once per document even when several paths require it, and
 * the first path that required it travels with it so the gap names a real file.
 *
 * @param {Object} scope - Parsed scope output
 * @param {string} repo - Resolved repository root
 * @returns {{notes: Map<string, string>, readme: Map<string, string>}}
 */
function collectRequirements(scope, repo) {
  const statuses = scope && typeof scope === 'object' ? scope.statuses : null;
  if (!statuses || typeof statuses !== 'object' || Array.isArray(statuses)) {
    throw new Error('scope.json has no statuses map');
  }
  const notes = new Map();
  const readme = new Map();
  for (const [file, status] of Object.entries(statuses)) {
    if (status !== 'A') continue;
    const changelog = SKILL_CHANGELOG.exec(file);
    if (changelog) {
      if (!notes.has(changelog[1])) notes.set(changelog[1], file);
      continue;
    }
    const doc = SKILL_DOC.exec(file);
    if (!doc) continue;
    const id = frontmatterName(repo, file) || doc[1];
    if (!notes.has(id)) notes.set(id, file);
    if (!readme.has(id)) readme.set(id, file);
  }
  return { notes, readme };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 6. MAIN
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function main() {
  const opts = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  const repo = path.resolve(opts.repo);
  const scope = readJson(path.resolve(opts.scope));
  const notes = openNotes(repo);
  const required = collectRequirements(scope, repo);
  let readme = '';
  try {
    readme = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo, 'README.md'), 'utf8');
  } catch {
    readme = '';
  }

  let missing = 0;
  for (const [id, file] of required.notes) {
    if (appearsIn(notes.text, id)) continue;
    console.log(`MISSING release-notes ${id} required by ${file}`);
    missing += 1;
  }
  for (const [id, file] of required.readme) {
    if (appearsIn(readme, id)) continue;
    console.log(`MISSING readme ${id} required by ${file}`);
    missing += 1;
  }
> build · deepseek-v4.1-flash

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/convergence/convergence.md:105:`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which is set to `maxIterations` when the config is created. .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/convergence/convergence.md:107:After the review floor, an expansive run that still holds an open or active node turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`, whichever candidate it was, `all_dimensions_clean` or a composite convergence vote. 115:The guarantees are absolute: divergent mode never changes the PASS/CONDITIONAL/FAIL mapping, never authorizes a fix, and never makes the review target writable. A successful pivot returns to the loop before `phase_synthesis`; verdict computation exists only inside `phase_synthesis`, where an active P0 or a required quality gate still failing at the terminal stop yields FAIL, active P1 or open search debt yields CONDITIONAL, and no active P0, active P1 or open search debt yields PASS. 1051:    dimensionCoverage: mergeDimensionCoverage(lineages),

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// A review lineage records what it covered in six surfaces. Merging them lets a guard
// read what the fan-out as a whole covered. Every merge below walks lineages in label
// order and sorts its output, so the merged registry never depends on arrival order.
const TRACEABILITY_STATUS_RANK = { fail: 5, blocked: 4, partial: 3, pass: 2, notApplicable: 1 };
const TRACEABILITY_UNDECIDED = new Set(['pending', 'deferred', 'skipped']);
const COVERAGE_BUCKETS = ['covered', 'ruledOut', 'deferred', 'blocked'];

function compareText(a, b) {
  const left = String(a);
  const right = String(b);
  if (left < right) return -1;
  return left > right ? 1 : 0;
}

function sortedUnion(lists) {
  return [...new Set(lists.flatMap((list) => (Array.isArray(list) ? list : [])))].sort(compareText);
}

function lineagesByLabel(lineageData) {
  return lineageData
    .filter((lineage) => lineage && lineage.registry && typeof lineage.registry === 'object')
    .sort((a, b) => compareText(a.label, b.label));
}

// A dimension counts as covered when any lineage covered it.
function mergeDimensionCoverage(lineages) {
  const merged = {};
  for (const { registry } of lineages) {
    const coverage = registry.dimensionCoverage;
    if (!coverage || typeof coverage !== 'object') continue;
    for (const [dimension, covered] of Object.entries(coverage)) {
      merged[dimension] = merged[dimension] === true || covered === true;
    }
  }
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(merged).sort(compareText).map((key) => [key, merged[key]]));
}

function traceabilityRank(status) {
  if (TRACEABILITY_UNDECIDED.has(status)) return 0;
  return TRACEABILITY_STATUS_RANK[status] ?? 0;
}

// The strictest status wins per protocol, and an undecided status never replaces a
// decided one; a tie keeps the first lineage in label order. The summary is then
// recomputed from the merged results with the reducer's own counting rules.
function mergeTraceability(lineages) {
  const byProtocol = new Map();
  for (const { registry } of lineages) {
    const results = registry.traceability?.results;
    if (!Array.isArray(results)) continue;
    for (const result of results) {
      if (!result || !result.protocolId) continue;
      const previous = byProtocol.get(result.protocolId);
      if (!previous || traceabilityRank(result.status) > traceabilityRank(previous.status)) {
        byProtocol.set(result.protocolId, result);
      }
    }
  }
  const results = [...byProtocol.keys()].sort(compareText).map((protocolId) => byProtocol.get(protocolId));

[0m$ [0mnl -ba README.md | sed -n '247,258p'
247	`/speckit:save` and `/speckit:resume` carry the work across sessions, as [Continuity](#continuity) describes. `/speckit:search` runs the trigger index and the ripgrep recipes over spec docs and skill docs. `/speckit:handoff` hands the work to a fresh session now, instead of waiting for the context limit.
   248	
   249	### Deep Research And Deep Review
   250	
   251	The deep loops run unattended and stop only when their own evidence says the work is done. `/deep:research` investigates a question one focused pass at a time. Results grow in one research document inside the spec folder. `/deep:review` audits code in passes, ranks every finding P0, P1 or P2 and never edits the code it reviews.
   252	
   253	Each pass runs in a fresh context against state kept on disk. A script, not the agent, decides when to stop. A review stays open while a P0 is active or a required check still fails, and it ends in a PASS, CONDITIONAL or FAIL verdict. A loop survives crashes and new sessions, because its progress lives in files.
   254	
   255	A loop can also spread across several AI CLIs at once. Each executor runs its own lineage, and for a review the strictest verdict wins the merge. A pool caps how many lineages run at once. A stall watchdog stops a lineage that stops making progress, and a cost cap bounds what each one spends. The [Deep Loop README](.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/README.md) describes both modes and the shared runtime.
   256	
   257	### CLI Orchestration
   258
[0m$ [0mnl -ba .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/changelog/v2.1.2.0.md | sed -n '15,24p'
15	A review that fans out to several lineages used to lose the coverage each lineage had recorded when the results were merged. The merge now carries those surfaces through, so the merged review shows what every lineage covered.
    16	
    17	&nbsp;
    18	
    19	## What's New at a Glance
    20	
    21	- **Dimension coverage is a union.** `dimensionCoverage` holds the dimensions every lineage covered, with sorted keys.
    22	- **Traceability keeps the strictest status.** Per protocol, `fail` beats `blocked`, `blocked` beats `partial`, `partial` beats `pass`, and `pass` beats `notApplicable`. An undecided status never replaces a decided one.
    23	- **Search and candidate coverage are merged.** `searchCoverage` holds sorted unions. Each `candidateCoverage` bucket lists the lineages that reported it.
    24	- **Divergence stays per lineage.** `divergenceByLineage` keeps each lineage's pivot state under its label. No merged `divergence` is written, because a merged current focus would be invented.

12:# Research Claude config dir (--claude-config-dir)
24:`runtime/scripts/dispatch-iteration.cjs` validates `--claude-config-dir` for both loop types. A review run on `cli-claude-code` without the flag fails with `missing required flag '--claude-config-dir'`. A research run accepts the flag. Any executor other than `cli-claude-code` refuses it with `flag '--claude-config-dir' is only valid with --executor cli-claude-code`.

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Merged Surfaces

`runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` `mergeReviewRegistries` merges each lineage's candidate coverage, dimension coverage and search coverage, and adds the merged surfaces to the returned registry.

### Merge Behavior

The surfaces are combined across lineages rather than read from one lineage alone, so the merged registry reflects coverage from every lineage in the fan-out.

Skip this redispatch when the failure reason is executor_missing, findings_not_enumerated or ledger_backing_missing. Those reasons are returned only after the narrative, state record and delta all exist, so the iteration already landed and a second leaf would refuse to overwrite it: treat it as still failing without a second dispatch.
